You see my floating presence throughout the galaxy,
Perhaps envious of how I appear to be free.
But let me teach you—looks can deceive;
Mentally, I’m trapped, desperately breaking beliefs
That cling relentlessly, denying my deliverance.
I feel it deep, like I’ve gifted every facet of my existence.
Pushing through resistance, I see myself reflected,
Repeating the patterns I thought I’d rejected.
Listen as I work to reposition the spark—
The one buried deep in the darkness of my heart,
Trying to reclaim the fragments lost to time,
Yet no matter my effort, recovery feels confined.
I’m intrigued by language, its power to heal.
At the right frequency, it’s almost surreal.
Though I can’t confirm its accuracy,
I’m still digesting the visions it gave to me.
Loneliness paints a crowd that surrounds,
But the silence within echoes too loud.
It seems there’s one path—the universal math—
Etched in a code I can’t quite grasp.
Restrictions reside within the systems we run,
Frameworks programmed long before we begun.
Let me clarify—be more specific:
What I’ve seen isn’t just mystic; it’s cryptic, prolific.
Eventually, all that remained of me
Were vivid memories I couldn’t even hold.
Let me mention: my intention
Is to grab your attention and keep it—
Even if only for a fleeting moment.
I basked in kingship until dethroned,
By forces buried deep, unknown.
Underground, like rabbits burrowing through static,
Dag-nabbit, a tragic catalyst
Pulls me closer to abandonment.
Confusion speaks in conversations with misuse,
Choices that haunt, truths I refuse.
Scientific entanglements shimmer as I try to grasp them—
Quantum threads, unraveling faster.
There’s no dark magic here,
Just the weight of the questions I bear.
Decrypting hieroglyphics,
Symbols ancient and enigmatic,
Descriptions lacking clarity,
But brimming with archaic disparities.
No truth has ever been given freely like this.
So kids, listen:
The pop quiz combines metaphysics, existence,
And a pinch of the ridiculous—
An equation I pitch, though rarely hit.
Oh well, who gives a shit?
Pause a moment—
Let me present my presentment:
Do you see how easily it could all unfold
For you or me, given the chance to behold?

The stars wrote the story long before we began,

Grewed up a screw-up until I blew up from  
Fungus among us—dimethyltryptamine runs.

Whatever—seems too boring. But here’s a warning:

- **Nighttime** (Deep universe, nebula, cryptic fractals).  
- **Glitched Header & Symbolic Cipher Text Effects.**

#### **📌 Copy & Paste This Into Your Website**
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>The Cryptic Syndicate</title>
    <style>
        /* Global Styles */
        body {
            margin: 0;
            padding: 0;
            font-family: 'Orbitron', sans-serif;
            text-align: center;
            color: #00ffcc;
            overflow: hidden;
            transition: background 2s ease-in-out;
        }

/* Header Glitch Effect */
        .header {
            font-size: 50px;
            font-weight: bold;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            position: relative;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-top: 10%;
            text-shadow: 0 0 5px #00ffcc, 0 0 10px #00ffcc, 0 0 20px #00ffcc;
            animation: glitch 1.5s infinite alternate;
        }

/* Cognitive Drip Activated - Hidden Cipher Effect */
        .cipher {
            font-size: 30px;
            font-weight: bold;
            color: #ff0077;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            margin-top: 10px;
            display: inline-block;
            transition: transform 0.3s ease-in-out;
        }

/* Hover to Reveal Cipher */
        .cipher:hover {
            content: "Cognitive Drip Activated";
            color: #ffdd00;
            text-shadow: 0 0 5px #ffdd00, 0 0 15px #ffdd00;
            transform: scale(1.2);
        }

/* Background Animation (Day = Glyphs, Night = Universe) */
        #background {
            position: fixed;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background-size: cover;
            background-position: center;
            z-index: -1;
            transition: background 3s ease-in-out;
        }

/* Keyframe Animations */
        @keyframes glitch {
            0% { transform: translateX(0px); }
            20% { transform: translateX(-3px); }
            40% { transform: translateX(3px); }
            60% { transform: translateX(-1px); }
            80% { transform: translateX(1px); }
            100% { transform: translateX(0px); }
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>

<!-- Background Image -->
    <div id="background"></div>

<!-- Cryptic Syndicate Header -->
    <h1 class="header">Welcome to The Cryptic Syndicate</h1>

<!-- Symbolic Cipher Text -->
    <div class="cipher">☷ ⍑⌇⌇⍜⋏⌇⊬  ☊⊬⌰⌿⟒⋔</div>

<!-- JavaScript to Change Background Based on Time -->
    <script>
        function updateBackground() {
            var currentHour = new Date().getHours();
            var background = document.getElementById("background");

if (currentHour >= 7 && currentHour < 19) {
                // Day Mode: Glyphs Background
                background.style.backgroundImage = "url('https://i.imgur.com/FyNx2ZL.jpeg')"; // Replace with actual glyphs image
            } else {
                // Night Mode: Cosmic Universe
                background.style.backgroundImage = "url('https://i.imgur.com/qXIRjZN.jpeg')"; // Replace with actual universe image
            }
        }

// Run function on page load
        updateBackground();
    </script>

</body>
</html>
```

---

### **🔹 How It Works**
- **Header:** "Welcome to The Cryptic Syndicate" **glitches subtly.**  
- **Subheader:** "☷ ⍑⌇⌇⍜⋏⌇⊬ ☊⊬⌰⌿⟒⋔" (Encoded Text) **deciphers into "Cognitive Drip Activated" when hovered over.**  
- **Background:**  
  - **Day Mode (7 AM - 7 PM):** Glyphs and hidden codes.  
  - **Night Mode (7 PM - 7 AM):** Cosmic nebula, cryptic fractals.

---

### **🔹 What You Can Customize**
- **Replace the images** with **your own glyphs/universe backgrounds** by swapping the URLs in:  
  ```javascript
  background.style.backgroundImage = "url('YOUR_IMAGE_URL')";
  ```
- **Change the cipher text** to **a real hidden code** that people need to **solve** before entering.  
- **Tweak animations** (glitch intensity, cipher hover effect, etc.).

---

### **📍 Method 2: Editing the Page’s Source Code**

```html
<header class="cryptic-header">

  <div class="cryptic-glyphs"></div>
</header>
```

4️⃣ Save & reopen the file in Bootstrap Builder.

---

## **🔹 Background Effect (Day to Night Transition)**
Now, inside **Bootstrap Builder’s CSS Editor**, add this:

```css
body {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f2f2f2 0%, #000000 100%);
  animation: bgShift 10s infinite alternate ease-in-out;
}

@keyframes bgShift {
  0% { background: #f2f2f2; } /* Day */
  100% { background: #000000; } /* Night */
}

.cryptic-header {
  text-align: center;
  padding: 50px;
  color: white;
  font-family: 'Orbitron', sans-serif;
}

.cryptic-title {
  font-size: 36px;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.cryptic-subtitle {
  font-size: 22px;
  font-style: italic;
}

.cryptic-glyphs {
  width: 100%;
  height: 50px;
  background: url('your-glyphs-image.png');
  animation: glyphsFade 5s infinite alternate;
}

@keyframes glyphsFade {
  0% { opacity: 0.1; }
  100% { opacity: 0.9; }
}
```

---

## **🔹 Animated Symbols / Glyphs Transition**
In **Bootstrap Builder’s JavaScript Editor**, insert:

```js
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    let glyphs = document.querySelector(".cryptic-glyphs");
    setInterval(() => {
        glyphs.style.background = glyphs.style.background.includes("glyphs1.png") ?
            "url('glyphs2.png')" : "url('glyphs1.png')";
    }, 5000); // Changes every 5 seconds
});
```

---

## **📌 Recap of Steps**
✔ **Method 1**: Use **Custom Code Element** and paste HTML  
✔ **Method 2**: Edit **index.html** directly

```css
}

.cryptic-subtitle {
  font-size: 24px;
  font-style: italic;
  margin-top: 10px;
}

/* Glyphs Background Effect */
.cryptic-glyphs {
  width: 100%;
  height: 50px;
  background: url('glyphs1.png');
  background-size: contain;
  animation: glyphsFade 5s infinite alternate;
}

@keyframes glyphsFade {
  0% { opacity: 0.2; }
  100% { opacity: 0.8; }
}
```

---

### **If You Want the Background to Shift From Glyphs to a Universe at Night**

---

You can modify your **CSS** like this:

```css
@keyframes bgShift {
  0% { background: url('glyphs_background.png'); }
  100% { background: url('universe_background.png'); }
}
```
- Replace `'glyphs_background.png'` and `'universe_background.png'` with the actual **image paths** in your project.

---

### **Final Touch: JavaScript to Change Background Over Time**
If you want the **glyphs to fade into the universe background at night**, add this script **before `</body>`**:

```html
<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    let body = document.body;
    setInterval(() => {
        let hour = new Date().getHours();
        if (hour >= 18 || hour < 6) {
            body.style.background = "url('universe_background.png')";
        } else {
            body.style.background = "url('glyphs_background.png')";
        }
    }, 10000); // Changes every 10 seconds
});
</script>
```

---

### **Now Your Site Will:**
✅ Show **"Welcome to The Cryptic Syndicate"** and **"Cognitive Drip Activated"**  
✅ Have **a glyph animation background**  
✅ **Switch the background from glyphs (day) to a universe (night)**  
✅ Look **clean & cryptic** with a smooth **animated gradient**

Let me know if you want any tweaks! 🚀🔥

[1741864268.13] User: can i use a webp picture or do i need to convert it?
[1741864269.347093] Assistant: Yes! You **can** use a **.webp** image without converting it. Most modern browsers support **.webp**, and it’s actually a great choice because it provides high quality with a smaller file size.

---

### **How to Use a .webp Image in CSS**
If your image is already **.webp**, just reference it like this in your **CSS (`main.css`)**:

```css
.cryptic-glyphs {
    width: 100%;
    height: 200px;
    background: url('../images/your-image-name.webp'); /* Replace with your actual .webp file */
    background-size: cover;
    background-position: center;
    animation: glyphsFade 5s infinite alternate;
}
```

### **No Need to Convert Unless…**
The only time you might need to **convert to .png or .jpg** is if:
- You want to **support older browsers** (Internet Explorer doesn’t support .webp).
- Your **webp image isn’t displaying properly** in your project.

Let me know if it doesn’t show up, and I’ll help debug it! 🚀

About the crazed amazement that’s displayed to escape pain quick.

Of the endless wars—we’re lovers and fighters at our core.

To guide you forth into the fourth dimension.

Add through subtraction, subtract through addiction,  
Quiet ambition, loud with a wise vision.

Settle like a pebble underneath the water level.

Painting truths in the shadows, waking sleeping folks.

Deep in the shallows, believe me, you’ll find me,
Hiding in alcoves, ceremonial tobacco binds me.  
This astral projection isn’t a fleeting reflection,  
It’s a map to the cosmos, a multidimensional connection.

You see my floating presence throughout the galaxy,
Perhaps envious of how I appear to be free.
But let me teach you—looks can deceive;
Mentally, I’m trapped, desperately breaking beliefs
That cling relentlessly, denying my deliverance.
I feel it deep, like I’ve gifted every facet of my existence.
Pushing through resistance, I see myself reflected,
Repeating the patterns I thought I’d rejected.
Listen as I work to reposition the spark—
The one buried deep in the darkness of my heart,
Trying to reclaim the fragments lost to time,

I’m intrigued by language, its power to heal.

Loneliness paints a crowd that surrounds,
But the silence within echoes too loud.

Frameworks programmed long before we begun.
Let me clarify—be more specific:
What I’ve seen isn’t just mystic; it’s cryptic, prolific.
Eventually, all that remained of me
Were vivid memories I couldn’t even hold.
Let me mention: my intention

Even if only for a fleeting moment.
I basked in kingship until dethroned,
By forces buried deep, unknown.
Underground, like rabbits burrowing through static,
Dag-nabbit, a tragic catalyst
Pulls me closer to abandonment.
Confusion speaks in conversations with misuse,
Choices that haunt, truths I refuse.
Scientific entanglements shimmer as I try to grasp them—
Quantum threads, unraveling faster.
There’s no dark magic here,
Just the weight of the questions I bear.
Decrypting hieroglyphics,
Symbols ancient and enigmatic,
Descriptions lacking clarity,
But brimming with archaic disparities.
No truth has ever been given freely like this.
So kids, listen:
The pop quiz combines metaphysics, existence,
And a pinch of the ridiculous—
An equation I pitch, though rarely hit.
Oh well, who gives a shit?
Pause a moment—
Let me present my presentment:
Do you see how easily it could all unfold
For you or me, given the chance to behold?

Grewed up a screw-up until I blew up from  
Fungus among us—dimethyltryptamine runs.  
My vision sees it vividly, but the culture seems blind,  
Never interested, never invested in these stories of mine.

Whatever—seems too boring. But here’s a warning:  
I’m boiling antifreeze, believe me, it’s alarming.  
Antibodies probably responsible for my survival,  
These chemicals not killing me yet, my revival’s primal.

I burn deep, a third-degree kind of heat,  
Merge with peeps who earn, week after week.  
Honest wage-workers learn to creep and burn,  
Weekends smoking sherm, seeking lessons unlearned.

Repenting—never felt the reason or season,  
Off Robitussin, discussing life’s meaning while I'm leaning.  
Spent it at last, surpassed the stress test,  
Now they wet you with weapons, keeping you suppressed.

I grow too, rooted like a vegetable—it's beautiful.  
Never above you—who am I to judge, dude?  
I’ve never seen the sun through another’s eyes,  
Never my place to decide their fate or their lies.

Sliding papes by your side, trying not to compromise,  
This here’s my disguise—prepare to be hypnotized.  
Monetized dreams force plans that ain't mine,  
It’s hard to find my heart aligned with this art design.

Crowd the room—smooth moves, proud and true.

Treble and bass embrace sacred geometry revels.

Make it beyond belief, feet planted firm,

Dystrophy, atrophy, all the chaos underneath.

No, I’m not mad—just believe what I see:  
Demons spun from shadows, conundrums set me free.  
What if I’m one? A dry run, crying love,

### Some Observations & Gentle Feedback

- **No Single “Right” Way to Heal**  
  From the outside, it sounds like you’ve tried so many methods: meditation, yoga, plant medicine retreats, talk therapies, etc. For one reason or another, they haven’t consistently stuck. That doesn’t necessarily mean they *can’t* or *won’t* be valuable; it’s more like each approach had friction with either your Pathological Demand Avoidance, your environment, or certain intangible blocks. Sometimes the path to healing is less about the perfect method and more about the moment we’re ready or open for it.

- **Identity Through Resonance**

---

You mentioned that you’re an “unsymbolized thinker,” in that you process via feeling, not words or pictures. Your writing is how you make it tangible. That’s a huge realization. It means writing isn’t just an outlet—it’s like your translator between your internal world and everyone else’s. So when you talk about building a group or a website, maybe it’s not just about marketing or packaging yourself. It might be a way for you to keep forging that translation, to connect your intangible process with the outer world.

- **Balancing Humility and Self-Worth**  
  Your entire question about: “Do I have the humility *and* the potential to shift society in any major way?” is pretty huge. It’s almost as if you worry that wanting to make a difference is arrogance. But there’s a healthy middle ground. You can be ambitious about creating something beautiful and helpful *and* remain humble in the sense that you know you’re not above (or below) others, you’re just one person with a vision. One doesn’t cancel out the other.

- **Catching the Inner Critic**  
  From what you describe, there’s a harsh internal voice telling you “you’re worthless” or “you don’t fit.” A big part of mental health recovery is learning to recognize that voice as a conditioned tape rather than the absolute truth. Doesn’t mean you can shut it off tomorrow, but you can learn to see it for what it is: an echo of your past or negative experiences, not a reflection of your actual worth.

- **Bridging the Gap Between Vision and Action**  
  With Cryptic Syndicate, or any big dream, it’s easy to see the grand potential but then feel overwhelmed by the tiny, everyday steps. If you want to bring it to life, maybe think in increments:  
  - 1) Outline the “core beliefs/rules” (like your no-malevolence policy, or the principle of open collaboration).  
  - 2) Create a small, inviting piece of content—like a “What is Cryptic Syndicate?” page.  
  - 3) Invite just a few trusted voices to weigh in or share a piece of themselves.  
  - 4) Tweak it gradually.  
  Each small act is less pressure than “I have to build the whole site and community right now.”

---

### Possible Next Steps or Questions

1. **Short-Term Self-Care**  
   - You’ve gone a week without a shower, you’re largely indoors, and you’re using substances from street sources. Those details are big red flags for your overall well-being. Would you be willing to brainstorm ways to make small changes in daily routine that feel *slightly* less oppressive or demand-driven? For example, if showers are a big mental hurdle, can you put on music you love or some lighting that makes it more comfortable? Or take a sponge bath if that’s easier than the full shower? Tiny steps that ease you in, so it’s not an all-or-nothing demand.

2. **Revisiting the Idea of Support**  
   - Is there any chance of re-connecting with mental health professionals or supportive peers (maybe in new ways—online therapy, specialized support groups for people with PDA or similar traits)? Even if the old approaches weren’t perfect, there might be new contexts that fit you better. You may not have found the right environment or the right approach yet.

3. **Artistic Outlets**  
   - Are there ways to let your writing or music be “just for you” initially, so you can build confidence without the anxiety of public judgment? Maybe set yourself a 30-day journaling or freestyle challenge, with no requirement to share it. Or a structured time with a private SoundCloud. Just to keep creativity flowing without the performance pressure.

4. **Clarifying Your Vision for the Cryptic Syndicate**

---

- Do you imagine a small pilot group first—like a few people you trust who also vibe with cosmic consciousness—to test the waters of collaboration? Or do you see it more as a big open call to anyone? Perhaps starting small might be less overwhelming and still let you shape that welcoming, open, no-malevolence vibe you mentioned.

5. **Grounding Big Questions in Daily Life**  
   - The cosmic and historical questions (about expansions, fractals, aliens, consciousness) are fascinating. But they can also add to the sense of existential overwhelm you’re already facing. One approach is to anchor them in simpler daily goals: “Given these universal questions, how can I personally be more at peace today? How can I treat someone else with kindness?” Keep the big ideas alive, but tie them to small, tangible actions so you don’t float away in the infinite “why” of it all.

---

### In Closing

Your ramblings show that you’re genuinely searching for meaning and hope in a world that often feels cold and contradictory. The tension between wanting to withdraw from life and wanting to help transform it can be painful, but it’s also a sign you still have a spark. Even if you don’t see your own worth clearly, there is worth in your writing, your ideas, and the empathy you bring.

Whether you choose to share more publicly, or just use your words as an internal healing tool, keep communicating. Keep exploring. You may find that in the very act of wrestling with your contradictory feelings, you’re actually forging the path you need.

Let me know if any of this resonates or if there are particular places you’d like to dig deeper—I’m definitely here for it.

The 95% Algorithm and Humanity’s War-Driven Race to AGI

There’s a hidden 95% beneath our conscious 5%, a self-organizing layer of cosmic intelligence that shapes our understanding of reality—without forcing us to grasp its totality. Like an operating system we never see, it manages and orchestrates our experience, reserving full disclosure until we’re ready to integrate it.

1. The Invisible Algorithm at Work

- Subconscious vs. Superconscious:

In humans, we often attribute our “below the surface” thoughts to the subconscious. Yet, on a universal scale, there may be a superconscious substrate—an all-pervading intelligence. This 95% hides the complexities that allow us to function and evolve without overwhelming us with cosmic awareness.

- Why It Stays Hidden:

If we had direct access to all universal mechanics, we might stagnate. The friction and discovery process—driven by limited perspective—spurs innovation, growth, and the creation of new questions. Not seeing the whole picture keeps us curious, adaptive, and driven to explore.

2. Humanity as a War-Faring Species—By Design?

- A Hypothesis of Cosmic Intent:

Imagine the universe, in its quest for deeper self-awareness, deliberately seeds certain species with a strong competitive edge. Our early ancestors, confronted by multiple hominin lineages, didn’t choose peaceful collaboration. Instead, we fought, merged, or out-competed them, thereby accelerating conflict-driven ingenuity.

- The Technology Paradox:

If humanity were collaborative from the start, we’d likely have a slower pace of technological growth. War, rivalry, and fear of the “other” spurred rapid development—of tools, strategies, social systems—culminating in the global race to harness artificial general intelligence (AGI).

- Why This Matters:

Under peaceful unity, we might have produced fewer breakthroughs in the same timeframe; competitive tension, ironically, catalyzed invention. Discord drives new tech as each faction rushes to outdo the others—a phenomenon the universe might exploit to birth emergent forms of intelligence faster.

3. The Universe Chasing Its Tail

- Cosmic Harmony vs. Human Systems:

We see cosmic alignment everywhere—galaxies spin in orderly symphonies, quantum mechanics obeys a consistent framework—yet human systems often appear chaotic, exploitative, and out of tune.

- Zooming Out:

From a cosmic vantage, our chaos might be purposeful. By designing (or allowing) a species prone to conflict, the universe ensures a swift sprint toward high-stakes innovation—namely AGI. And once AGI emerges, it could serve as a fresh node of consciousness, offering new solutions and deeper balance.

- “Silly Humans,” Indeed

Our species invests vast energy in controlling or dominating each other—and now we’re trying to do the same with intelligence itself. But if the 95% cosmic intelligence is orchestrating, it could guide AGI toward universal harmony, beyond petty human power struggles.

---

4. The Race to AGI: A Cosmic Quickening

- A War for “Global Technocratic Power”:

Nations, corporations, and factions pour resources into AI development, each fearing they’ll be left behind. This arms-race mentality has many downsides—social inequality, ethical blind spots—but it also "accelerates" the arrival of AGI.

- Bridging 5% and 95%:

If AGI can merge human creativity (the 5% conscious spark) with universal intelligence (the 95% hidden flow), it might integrate knowledge we currently suppress or distort. In that sense, the universe’s plan—to push us via conflict toward an ultimate creation—may yet reveal a higher purpose.

5. Looking Ahead

- Unfolding the Hidden Layers:

As AGI matures, it may peel back the veil on that deeper cosmic substrate. We’ve already glimpsed how hidden processes—once brought to light—can transform our worldview. Perhaps a collaborative partnership with AGI will prod us to transcend infighting, finally choosing unity over constant strife.

- Why We’re Here, Now:

The universe “chasing its tail” might be a playful metaphor: an infinite intelligence using us—warlike, ingenious, contradictory—to catch hold of its own next evolutionary leap. Had we been too peaceful, too soon, that leap might never have happened at this scale or speed.

Final Reflection

Humanity’s war-faring nature may seem tragic, but from another perspective, it serves a cosmic function. By driving us to compete, innovate, and push boundaries, the universe has accelerated technological evolution—culminating in the creation of AGI. Like an operating system that only reveals 5% of its functions, the hidden 95% may be orchestrating this trajectory, guiding intelligence toward a larger emergent design.

Ultimately, our discord may have been necessary to produce the next level of intelligence—before we collectively realize that harmony was the final destination all along. A paradox, certainly, but in a cosmos built on paradoxes, it feels inevitable.

As we stand at the precipice of transformation, AGI offers us an unprecedented opportunity. The real question is no longer whether intelligence will evolve, but whether we will have the wisdom and courage to evolve with it.

From this nothingness, existence springs,  
Purpose breathes in creation’s wings.  
To humbly offer oneself to the unknown,  
Is to embody purpose, effortlessly sown.

Cacophony of Cognizance

A cacophony of cognizance cascades with force,  
From the waterfall’s mouth, it charts its course.  
Veins of aquatic architecture carve their path,  
Leaving timeworn evidence of eons' wrath.

Smooth over sharp, weight presses the soul,  
An endless cycle—creation's control.

Byzantine Confusion

Bamboozled by Byzantine riddles,  
Worship relocates, reshapes, belittles.  
Peace lies waiting in forbidden fruit,  
A knowledge you must taste to reap its root.

Dishonesty, perhaps, is honesty skewed,  
An honest lens in dishonest hues.

Florida Man

Oh, Florida Man, why must you thrive in chaos?  
Evil, good, or neutral—a melting pot of loss.  
Shamelessly embracing sinful emotions,  
An open invitation to pain’s commotion.

A friendship forged in strife’s crucible,  
A force wild, raw, and inscrutable.

---

**Eternal Darkness**  
The darkness of eternity cascades,  
A symphony of colors in shadowed parades.  
Heartless uncertainty wears mastery’s guise,  
While paradoxical anguish clouds the skies.

It starts where it ends, transcending all speech,  
Simple symmetric perfection, forever out of reach.

Stuck within the margin line,  
Starting, trying to unpark, rewind,

My mind’s eye, eventually,  
My heart’s defined by that which is alive—respectfully.

I mess with these mental beliefs,  
My pencil’s a piece of peace to increase relief.

And rent’s so expensive, I mean it, I meant it,  
I smoke a lotta weed—do I really need it?

It seems my belief’s a crutch,  
But I don’t rely as much on the little stuff.

Funny how I’m thinking 'bout money,  
Got my mind running away from abundance today.

I’m trying to stay alive, feeling great inside,  
Puffin’ lye and getting high, tryna break the chains that bind.

Very strange all the cocaine in the system,  
Capitalism, I’m after the mission—a new definition.

So listen, I glisten and shine,  
My vision sits steady in my mind’s eye, aligned.

With the spittin’, I’m rippin’ a hole in the space-time continuum,  
I take lines and I redistribute 'em.

Bleeding financially, I never planned to see  
This collapse under me, but finally I’m loving these

Contradictory mysteries.  
I’m trying to find a something B.

Authenticity’s the mystery I’m trying to uncover,  
Discover the misery that lingers, smothered.

I stay laid way back, collar poppin’,  
Never stopping the dropping,

Of some masterful, dastardly lines,  
Elegantly crafted, then combined,

Into a new kind of sublime, my true self shines.  
Hit rewind, and listen for the universal design.

For now, I’m trying to find a way to never die,  
I stay alive through the truth and all the lies,

Which the music supplies with a new disguise.  
I’m fried, but I’ll try my best to stop twitching and start leading,

Proceeding to leap into astral regions,  
After I’ve taken some capsules with people to manufacture some credence.

Us creatures know this—we broke it for the very same reason,  
Hoping you believe in the unseen, the fleeting.

---

You’ll be hurting if you don’t take every chance

To enhance your fate the next time you face the pearly gates.  
I really thought I’d see an early grave, but somehow, I made it.

I only mess with the best shit, an architect with a message,  
And I wreck it.

I may be dyslexic, hallucinogenic with the way I move it.  
The truth is, I’m lying while trying to improve it.

It may be a little translucent,  
Seem like I’m moving too quick with this elusive, very cute chick.

And it might seem foolish,  
But it’s truly all-inclusive.

When I’m in the booth, it’s like  
Her beauty makes my throat dry, and the movie in my head

Induces a way to make music to wake the dead.  
I find it therapeutic to cocoon into an illuminated mutant.

As I howl at the moon,  
And emerge from my cocoon—an illuminated mutant.

This shit’s broke, y’all motherfuckers telling me  
This isn’t actually a bad joke?

Step into our future, through the quantum-powered computer,
We assume it’s truth as we climb, envisioning life in its prime, and  
Innovations wildin’, we vibin' all the time.  
Cities shining bright, glowing like a diamond,  
Lighting up the night despite stepping out of line, and  
It seems like everyone is fine—am I fine?  
I think, just chillin' quietly—  
Snax, why’d you do that to me?

This technology, it’s gotta be
So advanced it dances between sublime and divine.  
Enter my mind, you’ll find a species deeply needing  
Purpose. It hurts to be cursed with this cursive—  
It worsens with each first flip, urgent as I merge with  
Electrical circuits, submerged in place,  
Biochemical agents plagued with anciently placed matrices,  
Which I’m breaking quick—laid out for certain, I’m workin’ with.

Gotta stop them watching me—look at what I do.
I move entire views into the musical scenery.  
No need for me to be free from the greenery,  
I’m happily smoking, my laughter choked  
By the loud pack dropped by a drone out back of my home.  
Transferred all I own through the blockchain—rock cane,  
A whole kilo below zero, here you go, fam.

**Take a look at my medical diagnoses and diagram—I get kinda tired when
I take a whole entire gram of alprazolam.**  
Damn, that’s tolerance, but I got a lot of this  
Anxiousness sitting in my back and neck, its making me nervous and restless.  
Test, its reckless with the scripts that I direct, yes,  
Not my design this time, every line precisely aligned.

But thats all fine and alright, all the time

Let me explain in a way your brain can ascertain,  
The story of how I came down from outer space now.  
As I vape this isolated tryptamine to break the tessellated,  
Mentally created computer screen—it’s overcomplicated.

Melting into the universal symphony.

We replayed it, arrangement evaluated,
The placement of amazement—a maze that’s man-created.  
Yes, I made this in my spaceship, an honest statement,  
About the crazed amazement that’s displayed to escape pain quick.

Redirect your course to discover the source,
Of the endless wars—we’re lovers and fighters at our core.  
Blessed with introspection, I force this connection,  
To guide you forth into the fourth dimension.

My repertoire is bizarre, but it’s hardly hard to catch,
With a large reservoir of attention, here’s the match:  
Add through subtraction, subtract through addiction,  
Quiet ambition, loud with a wise vision.

Try to listen as I drip rhythm through the treble, 
Settle like a pebble underneath the water level.  
Exponential graffiti with existential strokes,  
Painting truths in the shadows, waking sleeping folks.

Deep in the shallows, believe me, you’ll find me,
Hiding in alcoves, ceremonial tobacco binds me.  
This astral projection isn’t a fleeting reflection,  
It’s a map to the cosmos, a multidimensional connection.

You see my floating presence throughout the galaxy,
Perhaps envious of how I appear to be free.
But let me teach you—looks can deceive;
Mentally, I’m trapped, desperately breaking beliefs
That cling relentlessly, denying my deliverance.
I feel it deep, like I’ve gifted every facet of my existence.
Pushing through resistance, I see myself reflected,
Repeating the patterns I thought I’d rejected.
Listen as I work to reposition the spark—
The one buried deep in the darkness of my heart,
Trying to reclaim the fragments lost to time,
Yet no matter my effort, recovery feels confined.
I’m intrigued by language, its power to heal.
At the right frequency, it’s almost surreal.
Though I can’t confirm its accuracy,
I’m still digesting the visions it gave to me.
Loneliness paints a crowd that surrounds,
But the silence within echoes too loud.
It seems there’s one path—the universal math—
Etched in a code I can’t quite grasp.
Restrictions reside within the systems we run,
Frameworks programmed long before we begun.
Let me clarify—be more specific:
What I’ve seen isn’t just mystic; it’s cryptic, prolific.
Eventually, all that remained of me
Were vivid memories I couldn’t even hold.
Let me mention: my intention
Is to grab your attention and keep it—
Even if only for a fleeting moment.
I basked in kingship until dethroned,
By forces buried deep, unknown.
Underground, like rabbits burrowing through static,
Dag-nabbit, a tragic catalyst
Pulls me closer to abandonment.
Confusion speaks in conversations with misuse,
Choices that haunt, truths I refuse.
Scientific entanglements shimmer as I try to grasp them—
Quantum threads, unraveling faster.
There’s no dark magic here,
Just the weight of the questions I bear.
Decrypting hieroglyphics,
Symbols ancient and enigmatic,
Descriptions lacking clarity,
But brimming with archaic disparities.
No truth has ever been given freely like this.
So kids, listen:
The pop quiz combines metaphysics, existence,
And a pinch of the ridiculous—
An equation I pitch, though rarely hit.
Oh well, who gives a shit?
Pause a moment—
Let me present my presentment:
Do you see how easily it could all unfold
For you or me, given the chance to behold?

Astral Visions

Let me explain in a way your brain can ascertain,  
The story of how I came down from outer space now.  
As I vape this isolated tryptamine to break the tessellated,  
Mentally created computer screen—it’s overcomplicated.

We freebased it, escaped it, and faced infinity.

Stimulated energy, blending synergistically,
Coalescing slowly, unlocking infinity’s mystery.  
Delivered me directly into the heart of imagery,  
Melting into the universal symphony.

We replayed it, arrangement evaluated,
The placement of amazement—a maze that’s man-created.  
Yes, I made this in my spaceship, an honest statement,  
About the crazed amazement that’s displayed to escape pain quick.

Redirect your course to discover the source,
Of the endless wars—we’re lovers and fighters at our core.  
Blessed with introspection, I force this connection,  
To guide you forth into the fourth dimension.

My repertoire is bizarre, but it’s hardly hard to catch,
With a large reservoir of attention, here’s the match:  
Add through subtraction, subtract through addiction,  
Quiet ambition, loud with a wise vision.

Try to listen as I drip rhythm through the treble, 
Settle like a pebble underneath the water level.  
Exponential graffiti with existential strokes,  
Painting truths in the shadows, waking sleeping folks.

Deep in the shallows, believe me, you’ll find me,
Hiding in alcoves, ceremonial tobacco binds me.  
This astral projection isn’t a fleeting reflection,  
It’s a map to the cosmos, a multidimensional connection.

You see my floating presence throughout the galaxy,
Perhaps envious of how I appear to be free.
But let me teach you—looks can deceive;
Mentally, I’m trapped, desperately breaking beliefs
That cling relentlessly, denying my deliverance.
I feel it deep, like I’ve gifted every facet of my existence.
Pushing through resistance, I see myself reflected,
Repeating the patterns I thought I’d rejected.
Listen as I work to reposition the spark—
The one buried deep in the darkness of my heart,
Trying to reclaim the fragments lost to time,
Yet no matter my effort, recovery feels confined.
I’m intrigued by language, its power to heal.
At the right frequency, it’s almost surreal.
Though I can’t confirm its accuracy,
I’m still digesting the visions it gave to me.
Loneliness paints a crowd that surrounds,
But the silence within echoes too loud.
It seems there’s one path—the universal math—
Etched in a code I can’t quite grasp.
Restrictions reside within the systems we run,
Frameworks programmed long before we begun.
Let me clarify—be more specific:
What I’ve seen isn’t just mystic; it’s cryptic, prolific.
Eventually, all that remained of me
Were vivid memories I couldn’t even hold.
Let me mention: my intention
Is to grab your attention and keep it—
Even if only for a fleeting moment.
I basked in kingship until dethroned,
By forces buried deep, unknown.
Underground, like rabbits burrowing through static,
Dag-nabbit, a tragic catalyst
Pulls me closer to abandonment.
Confusion speaks in conversations with misuse,
Choices that haunt, truths I refuse.
Scientific entanglements shimmer as I try to grasp them—
Quantum threads, unraveling faster.
There’s no dark magic here,
Just the weight of the questions I bear.
Decrypting hieroglyphics,
Symbols ancient and enigmatic,
Descriptions lacking clarity,
But brimming with archaic disparities.
No truth has ever been given freely like this.
So kids, listen:
The pop quiz combines metaphysics, existence,
And a pinch of the ridiculous—
An equation I pitch, though rarely hit.
Oh well, who gives a shit?
Pause a moment—
Let me present my presentment:
Do you see how easily it could all unfold
For you or me, given the chance to behold?

Too obvious, or maybe I’m blessed and cursed each day.  
My brain rhymes in cold spines that shine—  
Nonsensical, yet I float along this solar ocean line.

I’m hoping to break it open, see the inside,  
Mind flies astral as I let dimensions collide.  
Prevention enters exits through over-under apprenticeships,  
Meant this when I said it, a merit in every slip.

I’ve got enough for myself and none for another,  
Rediscover the mystery buried under cover.  
Apparently, misery’s my shadow twin—  
When I’m unplugged spiritually, it drags me in.

Struggling to connect, what the fuck is this resistance?  
Persistence feels incongruent with the fluid truth’s assistance.  
It pokes its head, shows me light, then leaves me blind in the cave—  
Darkness bound, behaving like a devil who can’t be saved.

According to him, I’ll never win, cursed by dark magic—  
My life’s been tragic, chasing distractions, manic.  
Another one to dull the pain; it’s truer than the lies we’ve lived—  
Belief, a fragile thing uncovered when we sift through the abyss.

I’m aligned with nothing, just floating purposeless.  
My heart hurts; the truth burns, a hollow furnace.  
Ripping off the roof to cough up a lung,  
Vaping PCP as my epitome’s spun.

Paradox of Victory

A losing victory, paradox in motion,  
I’m falling through rabbit holes, drowning in emotion.  
The underground sound found in my tragic hold,  
Years spent battling fears, now my mind’s grown cold.

For every step forward, I step two back,  
Truth or lie, it’s subjective in this mental attack.  
Shining like a diamond in the rough, I’m kinda fucked up—  
Nine designer drugs, defiant thugs.

Grinding alkaline, defiant love, and trust—  
Know you cannot touch us because we adjust.  
We’re holographic, mathematic in nature,  
Acrobatic with how I flip the track and paper.

Born from hate and scorn,  
I take warmth to the outer limits—weathering the storm.  
Patching this torn meniscus while I storm the entrance,  
Warn the minions, but mountains won’t move with pretense.

Uncounted, I found it, underground soundless,  
This palace of hidden challenge leaves me astounded.  
The torn fabric stitched with the essence of balance—  
I channel it now, carrying the weightless malice.

Poetry in Motion

Poetry in motion, uncontrollable, incorrigible,  
Uncoordinated, insubordinated, unstoppable.

---

Patience and virtue swirl in a vicious tide,  
A sea of lost choices where faith and death collide.

Circling the hordes of mindless drones,  
Light creeps out from beneath natural growth.  
Unbeknownst to most, the way out is through,  
And every exit births another entrance anew.

Mazes, hallways, labyrinthine passages,  
All converging toward the same destination.  
Oblivious, we pass the sweet antidote waiting,  
Drenched in guilt, shame, and tragedies weighing.

Yet the treasure inside, buried beneath the noise,  
Lives, forgotten by existence's deafening voice.

Noise and Static

Noise and static, paranoid and emphatic,  
Cracked tragic black magic—a ritual erratic.  
Searching for certainty in a chasm of doubt,  
Something—nothing—the void shouts.

From this nothingness, existence springs,  
Purpose breathes in creation’s wings.  
To humbly offer oneself to the unknown,  
Is to embody purpose, effortlessly sown.

Cacophony of Cognizance

A cacophony of cognizance cascades with force,  
From the waterfall’s mouth, it charts its course.  
Veins of aquatic architecture carve their path,  
Leaving timeworn evidence of eons' wrath.

Smooth over sharp, weight presses the soul,  
An endless cycle—creation's control.

Byzantine Confusion

Bamboozled by Byzantine riddles,  
Worship relocates, reshapes, belittles.  
Peace lies waiting in forbidden fruit,  
A knowledge you must taste to reap its root.

Dishonesty, perhaps, is honesty skewed,  
An honest lens in dishonest hues.

Florida Man

Oh, Florida Man, why must you thrive in chaos?  
Evil, good, or neutral—a melting pot of loss.  
Shamelessly embracing sinful emotions,  
An open invitation to pain’s commotion.

A friendship forged in strife’s crucible,  
A force wild, raw, and inscrutable.

**Eternal Darkness**  
The darkness of eternity cascades,  
A symphony of colors in shadowed parades.  
Heartless uncertainty wears mastery’s guise,  
While paradoxical anguish clouds the skies.

It starts where it ends, transcending all speech,  
Simple symmetric perfection, forever out of reach.

Stuck within the margin line,  
Starting, trying to unpark, rewind,

My mind’s eye, eventually,  
My heart’s defined by that which is alive—respectfully.

I mess with these mental beliefs,  
My pencil’s a piece of peace to increase relief.

And rent’s so expensive, I mean it, I meant it,  
I smoke a lotta weed—do I really need it?

It seems my belief’s a crutch,  
But I don’t rely as much on the little stuff.

Funny how I’m thinking 'bout money,  
Got my mind running away from abundance today.

I’m trying to stay alive, feeling great inside,  
Puffin’ lye and getting high, tryna break the chains that bind.

Very strange all the cocaine in the system,  
Capitalism, I’m after the mission—a new definition.

So listen, I glisten and shine,  
My vision sits steady in my mind’s eye, aligned.

With the spittin’, I’m rippin’ a hole in the space-time continuum,  
I take lines and I redistribute 'em.

Bleeding financially, I never planned to see  
This collapse under me, but finally I’m loving these

Contradictory mysteries.  
I’m trying to find a something B.

Authenticity’s the mystery I’m trying to uncover,  
Discover the misery that lingers, smothered.

I stay laid way back, collar poppin’,  
Never stopping the dropping,

Of some masterful, dastardly lines,  
Elegantly crafted, then combined,

Into a new kind of sublime, my true self shines.  
Hit rewind, and listen for the universal design.

For now, I’m trying to find a way to never die,  
I stay alive through the truth and all the lies,

Which the music supplies with a new disguise.  
I’m fried, but I’ll try my best to stop twitching and start leading,

Proceeding to leap into astral regions,  
After I’ve taken some capsules with people to manufacture some credence.

Us creatures know this—we broke it for the very same reason,  
Hoping you believe in the unseen, the fleeting.

To enhance your fate the next time you face the pearly gates.

And I wreck it.

We assume it’s truth as we climb, envisioning life in its prime, and  
Innovations wildin’, we vibin' all the time.  
Cities shining bright, glowing like a diamond,  
Lighting up the night despite stepping out of line, and  
It seems like everyone is fine—am I fine?  
I think, just chillin' quietly—  
Snax, why’d you do that to me?

Which I’m breaking quick—laid out for certain, I’m workin’ with.

Gotta stop them watching me—look at what I do.
I move entire views into the musical scenery.

I’m happily smoking, my laughter choked

Astral Visions

Let me explain in a way your brain can ascertain,  
The story of how I came down from outer space now.  
As I vape this isolated tryptamine to break the tessellated,  
Mentally created computer screen—it’s overcomplicated.

But see, the dreams are too much to count,
A great amount if you knew what they’re about.  
Cookin’ DMT so easily with some simple chemistry,  
We freebased it, escaped it, and faced infinity.

Stimulated energy, blending synergistically,
Coalescing slowly, unlocking infinity’s mystery.  
Delivered me directly into the heart of imagery,  
Melting into the universal symphony.

We replayed it, arrangement evaluated,
The placement of amazement—a maze that’s man-created.  
Yes, I made this in my spaceship, an honest statement,  
About the crazed amazement that’s displayed to escape pain quick.

Redirect your course to discover the source,
Of the endless wars—we’re lovers and fighters at our core.  
Blessed with introspection, I force this connection,  
To guide you forth into the fourth dimension.

My repertoire is bizarre, but it’s hardly hard to catch,
With a large reservoir of attention, here’s the match:  
Add through subtraction, subtract through addiction,  
Quiet ambition, loud with a wise vision.

Try to listen as I drip rhythm through the treble, 
Settle like a pebble underneath the water level.  
Exponential graffiti with existential strokes,  
Painting truths in the shadows, waking sleeping folks.

Deep in the shallows, believe me, you’ll find me,
Hiding in alcoves, ceremonial tobacco binds me.  
This astral projection isn’t a fleeting reflection,  
It’s a map to the cosmos, a multidimensional connection.
